    "body": "Repository: https://github.com/MJS-708/MRManalyzeR\n\nMRManalyzeR provides reproducible post-acquisition processing of targeted LC–MS/MS metabolomics results exported from Waters TargetLynx or Skyline, or supplied as a generic sample-by-analyte matrix. It integrates quantitative result tables with sample and analyte metadata, performs targeted-assay-specific quality control and matrix processing, returns a struct::DatasetExperiment, and renders reproducible data-quality and statistical reports.\n\nAll core processing and analysis steps are available through exported R functions. Complete workflows can additionally be executed from a documented YAML configuration, making the package accessible to laboratory users with limited R programming experience while retaining a fully programmatic interface for advanced users and reproducible pipelines.\n\nMRManalyzeR begins with reviewed vendor result tables. Chromatographic peak detection, peak integration and calibration-curve fitting from raw mass-spectrometry data are outside its scope. Within Bioconductor, this distinguishes it from xcms, which processes raw untargeted LC–MS data; pmp, which provides general peak-matrix preprocessing; and NormalyzerDE, which evaluates normalisation methods and differential analysis for quantitative LC–MS matrices. MRManalyzeR instead focuses on metadata-aware post-acquisition processing, accurate quantification adjustments, quality control and reporting for reviewed targeted small-molecule assays.\n\nIt also differs from chromatogram-level targeted-MRM software such as MRMkit and MRMQuant, which automate processing and quantitation from instrument data. MRManalyzeR starts from the reviewed quantitative outputs produced by vendor software and supports subsequent study-level quality control, processing, statistical analysis and reporting.\n\nMRManalyzeR is submitted as a Software package. R CMD check --as-cran and BiocCheck pass with zero errors and zero warnings.\n\nAI disclosure: Portions of this package were developed with assistance from Anthropic Claude. All AI-assisted code has been reviewed and tested by the maintainer, who accepts responsibility for its correctness, provenance and licensing.",

    "body": "Update the following URL to point to the GitHub repository of\nthe package you wish to submit to _Bioconductor_\n\n- Repository: https://github.com/RforMassSpectrometry/SpectraStash\n\n*Note*: this package provides implementations for language-agnostic storage/serialization formats using the *MsStash* (submitted with issue #70) for objects defined in the *Spectra* Bioconductor package. The package provides also support for storage of MS data objects in *alabaster* format.",
